Ignore. Now design the MPI Cholesky.\n\nWe need to maintain semantics: output the L matrix in row-major n*n with upper triangle zeroed, print results via print_results(A, \"CholeskyL\") on all data. Validation. Timing measured.\n\nDesign options for distributed-memory Cholesky:\n\nGiven only use MPI, no BLAS. We can implement a distributed blocked Cholesky (right-looking or left-looking) with row-block distribution, communication of panels via MPI_Bcast. Aim for performance.\n\nSimplest high-performance approach: Blocked, right-looking Cholesky with 2D block-cyclic distribution? That's more complex. Row-block distribution with 1D column panels: For each panel of width nb, factor the diagonal block, then broadcast the panel rows to all processes, then each process updates its trailing rows (rows assigned to it) with a rank-nb update (GEMM). This is a standard \"row-block distributed, broadcast panel\" algorithm. Requirement: each process owns a contiguous block of rows (possibly all rows after its start), and needs access to the panel (columns k..k+nb-1) of all rows i>=k. Solutions for L panel computation for rows owned by other processes: the diagonal block must be computed first by the owner of the rows; then solve for panel below. Those rows belong to other processes. Approach: All rows of the panel (i>=k) are computed by distributing: for the panel, each process computes its own rows' panel entries, but to do so needs L[k:k+nb, k:k+nb] (diagonal block, factorized), owned by one process. So: owner of diagonal block factors it, broadcasts diagonal block to all; then each process computes its own panel rows (the \"triangular solve\" part): for each row in its range >= k+nb, needs sum over previous columns <k, i.e. A[i, k:k+nb] -= L[i, 0:k] * L[k:k+nb, 0:k]^T, then solve with triangular block. This requires L[i, 0:k] local (owned by the process, since row-distributed, yes each row's full data local), and L[k:k+nb, 0:k] which are the panel rows from the diagonal block process — those are rows k..k+nb-1, columns 0..k-1. Those have to be broadcast. So owner of diagonal block also broadcasts its panel rows' previous columns (width k). Then each process updates its rows, then solves against diagonal block.\n\nThen broadcast the newly computed panel rows (all rows i>=k) so that everyone can do the trailing GEMM? Each row is owned by exactly one process; trailing update is A[i, j] -= L[i, panel] * L[j, panel]^T for all rows i, columns j>=k+nb, but only lower part. If rows are distributed, process owning row i needs L[i,panel] (local, computed) and L[j,panel] for all j in its column range (all rows j>=k+nb). So need the entire panel L[k:, k:k+nb] available at each process — broadcast (gather then Bcast or Allgatherv). So yes:\n\nRight-looking blocked algorithm:\n- Distribution: contiguous row blocks among P processes. Each process owns rows [rstart, rend). Actually for load balance with the triangular structure, row blocks of equal size — workload declines with row, but contiguous equal splitting gives imbalance. Work for row i ~ ... in the blocked right-looking, panel k: rows i>k each do (n-k) flops per column... Total work for row i ~ sum over j<=i ... ~ roughly i*n - i²/2 flops. This peaks at i=n with n²/2. So monotonic increasing with i. So work is increasing with i. Balanced split: first proc should own more rows than last. Since row blocks are contiguous, easy.\n\n   Then trailing update: local rows i>=k+kb, columns j from k+kb..n-1 (lower triangle): A[i, j] -= sum_{t=0}^{kb-1} Lnew[i, k+t] * Lnew[j, k+t]. This is a GEMM of local rows (r × kb) times (kb × columns for all rows j). But Lnew[j, ...] needed for j >= i (lower triangle), which spans all global rows. Full panel buffer (n-k)×kb available, so use only rows j>=i.\n\n   This trailing GEMM is the bulk of flops. Writing an efficient custom GEMM with no BLAS: use loop ordering with register blocking. That's a rank-1 update (DGER) over the local row-block region. Complexity: memory traffic O(n²kb) per panel but with good cache blocking it's ok. Actually DGER over the local rows: for each t: for each local row i: for j from i..n: A[i,j] -= Li_t * panel[j,t]. This reads/writes A row segment and reads panel column. Panel column length n, streamed repeatedly for each local row → memory traffic O(local_rows * kb * n) = O(n²kb/P). With kb=64, that's large but cache: panel column (n doubles ~ 4KB for n=512, 32KB for n=2048) fits L2. The A row segments streamed from memory, each panel t pass re-reads A. Standard approach: pack local panel block (local rows × kb) into contiguous buffer, pack B (panel block for columns j from local column start .. n, rows ≥ ...). Then do a tiled GEMM with micro-kernels. This is significant work but doable.\n\nGiven constraints (no new deps, must compile, correctness), a moderately optimized tiled GEMM is fine. We should ensure decent performance.\n\nAlternatively, a simpler but still scalable approach: 2D SUMMA-like? Too complex.\n\nLet's consider a simpler design that gets good performance without writing full GEMM micro-kernels: right-looking with nb tuned (e.g., 128) and trailing update as tiled loops:\n\nfor each local row block ir (tile of e.g. 8 rows):\n  for each column tile jc:\n     C_tile = A[ir, jc]\n     for t in 0..kb-1: dger... \nHmm.\n\nActually pure loop:\nfor (i local) {\n  const double* Li = &A[i*n];\n  for (t = 0; t < kb; ++t) {\n     double lit = Lnew[i][t]; // new panel value\n     const double* bt = panel_T + t*n; // contiguous over j\n     for (j = jstart; j < n; ++j) Li[j] -= lit * bt[j];\n  }\n}\nThis is a rank-1 update with inner loop over contiguous j, vectorizable, but A row i is streamed kb times → traffic local_rows*kb*n*8 bytes. For n=4096, P=8: local_rows=512, kb=64 → 512*64*4096*8 = 1 GB per panel; panels=64 → 68 GB traffic. That's maybe 3-5 s at 20GB/s; flops=2/3 n³=45 GFLOP → ~10 GFLOPS. Not great but acceptable? We'll implement:\n\n```\nvoid updateTrailing(double* A, const std::vector<int>& l2g, size_t startLocal,\n                    size_t threshold, size_t kcol, size_t n, size_t kb,\n                    const double* pan, const double* Blocal)\n```\nWhere rows: local rows i (startLocal..lrows-1) with global gi = l2g[i] >= threshold. Columns from kcol = k+kb to n-1. Update A[i*n + j] -= sum_t Blocal[i*kb+t] * pan[(gj-threshold)*kb + t], for j >= gi.\n\nTo exploit symmetry? We only update lower triangle. Use tiling:\n\n```\nconst size_t MR = 8, NC = 128;\nfor each local row block I of MR rows:\n   size_t gmax = max global row in block\n   for jt from kcol to n step NC:\n      nc = min(NC, n-jt)\n      if (jt + nc <= first global row of block) continue; // entirely above diag\n      // compute into C tile\n      // for each row r in I:\n      //   jstart = max(gi_r, jt) etc.\n```\nThe diagonal-crossing handling per row complicates vectorization. Simplify: for each column tile J, rows tiles I; skip if J end <= gi of first row; else compute full tile but for row r only j >= gi_r overwrite. We can compute full tile in a stack C[MR][NC] then add only valid entries. Waste at diagonal ~ MR*NC per block-tile pair, total ~ (n/MR)*(n/NC)*MR*NC/2 = n²/2 negligible vs n³.\n\nActually computing full tiles including upper entries where j<gi would double count? We only *store* j>=gi. Computing C for all j uses pan row j which exists (j>=kcol>...). pan includes all rows. Blocal row r valid. Fine. So:\n\n```\ndouble C[MR][NC]; // 8*128*8=8KB\ninit C=0? then accumulate outer products: for t: for r: lit = Blocal[r*kb+t]; if lit != 0 ... for j: C[r][j] -= lit*pan[(jglobal...)*kb+t]\n```\nHmm pan rows j indexed from threshold; for column tile starting jt, the pan row index = jt - threshold + jj. Contiguous in j? pan is row-major kb per row, so pan[row*kb + t] has stride kb over rows for fixed t. The inner loop over j for fixed t reads pan[(row)*kb + t] with stride kb — not unit stride → bad vectorization. Better store pan transposed: panT[t*nrem + row] so contiguous over rows j. Then for fixed t, panT[t*nrem + (jt-threshold) + jj] contiguous over jj. Then C[r][jj] -= lit * panT_row[jj]: inner over jj contiguous both → vectorizes. Good. So maintain pan as transposed: panT of size kb * nrem (column-major with leading dim nrem). When copying from rbuf (row-major rows × kb), transpose during the paste. Fine.\n\nAnd Blocal (local new panel values) stored row-major local_rows × kb, accessed Blocal[r*kb + t] with t inner — good for outer loop over t.\n\nGEMM loop:\n```\nfor row tile (r0..r0+mr):\n   for col tile (jt..jt+nc):\n      if (jt+nc <= l2g[r0]) continue; // all above diagonal for all rows in tile? Actually A += C where C accumulated negative. Fine. The inner jj loop vectorizes.\n\nBut `mr` could be up to 8; the inner loop over t with r loop inside; compiler can unroll r. Use MPI_COMM_WORLD directly.\n\nLet me write the algorithm code:\n\n```\nbool choleskyDecompositionMPI(std::vector<double>& A, size_t n, size_t nb,\n                              const std::vector<int>& l2g, int rank, int P,\n                              int& failIndex) {\n    const size_t lrows = l2g.size();\n    size_t nremMax = n; // panT buffers sized nb*n\n    std::vector<double> panT(nb * n);      // transposed panel rows for all global rows >= threshold\n    std::vector<double> rbuf(nb * n);      // gathered row-major\n    std::vector<double> Blocal(lrows * nb, 0.0); // new panel values for local rows\n    std::vector<double> dd(nb * n, 0.0);   // broadcast panel rows: kb × (k+kb) (max kb*(k+kb) <= nb*n)\n    std::vector<int> counts(P), displs(P);\n\n    bool ok = true;\n    failIndex = -1;\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += nb) {\n        const size_t kb = std::min(nb, n - k);\n        const size_t diagBlock = k / nb;\n        const int owner = int(diagBlock % P);\n\n        // 1. Diagonal block factorization on owner\n        bool localOk = true;\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            // find local index of row k\n            size_t rk = lower_bound(l2g, k);  // local index\n            // ensure rows k..k+kb-1 contiguous local (they are, same block)\n            for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb && localOk; ++jj) {\n                size_t j = k + jj;\n                double sum = 0.0;\n                for (size_t t = 0; t < j; ++t) {\n                    double v = A[(rk+jj)*n + t];\n                    sum += v*v;\n                }\n                double val = A[(rk+jj)*n + j] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) {\n                    localOk = false;\n                    failIndex = int(j);\n                    break;\n                }\n                double d = sqrt(val);\n                A[(rk+jj)*n + j] = d;\n                for (size_t ii = jj+1; ii < kb; ++ii) {\n                    double s2 = 0.0;\n                    for (size_t t = 0; t < j; ++t)\n                        s2 += A[(rk+ii)*n + t] * A[(rk+jj)*n + t];\n                    A[(rk+ii)*n + j] = (A[(rk+ii)*n + j] - s2) / d;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        int okFlag = localOk ? 1 : 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&okFlag, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (!okFlag) {\n            MPI_Bcast(&failIndex, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            ok = false;\n            break;\n        }\n        // 2. Broadcast dd panel rows k..k+kb-1, columns 0..k+kb-1\n        size_t rowLen = k + kb;\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            size_t rk = lower_bound(l2g,k);\n            for (ii<kb) memcpy(&dd[ii*rowLen], &A[(rk+ii)*n], rowLen*sizeof(double));\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(dd.data(), int(kb*rowLen), MPI_DOUBLE, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        // 3. compute local panel for rows >= k+kb\n        size_t thr = k + kb;\n        size_t start = lower_bound(l2g, thr); // local index\n        for (size_t r = start; r < lrows; ++r) {\n            double* Ai = &A[r*n];\n            double* Bl = &Blocal[r*nb];\n            for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb; ++jj) {\n                size_t j = k + jj;\n                double s = 0.0;\n                for (size_t t = k; t < j; ++t)\n                    s += Ai[t] * dd[(j-k)*rowLen + t];\n                Ai[j] = (Ai[j] - s) / dd[(j-k)*rowLen + j];\n                Bl[jj] = Ai[j];\n            }\n        }\n        // 4. allgatherv rows >= thr\n        size_t lcnt = lrows - start;\n        counts...\n        for p: counts[p] = countRowsFromRank(p, thr, n, nb, P); displs prefix\n        MPI_Allgatherv(&Blocal[start*nb], int(lcnt*kb), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                       rbuf.data(), cntk, displk, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        // transpose paste into panT indexed 0..nrem-1 where nrem=n-thr\n        size_t nrem = n - thr;\n        for p: c=displs[p]; for blocks b=p,p+P: s0..e; g from max(s0,thr)..e:\n             for t: panT[t*nrem + (g-thr)] = rbuf[c*kb+t];\n        // 5. trailing GEMM for local rows start..lrows and columns >= gi\n        ...\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n```\n\nWait, step 1 diagonal factor: uses `for t < j` all columns from 0; but for j in panel, columns t in [k, j-1] have been updated correctly by previous panels; columns t<k too. Original does `(A[i,j] - sum)` where A[i,j] is the original generated value (never modified before its row i is processed, since updates are only made to row i when processing row i; other rows' entries untouched). That's a left-looking algorithm: to compute row i, need L[j,k] for j<i (previous rows) and L[i,k] k<j (current row, already computed within row). If we distribute rows across ranks, computing row i requires previous rows j<i which are distributed. Each row i computation needs L[j, *] for all j<i — that's the entire previous rows. Broadcasting each computed row to everyone, then each rank computes its owned rows. We can batch: broadcast a panel of rows (nb rows) and then all processes compute the next panel's rows using previous panel data. A left-looking blocked algorithm:\n\nFor panel K (rows k..k+nb-1): each row i in panel needs L[j, 0..i-1] for all j<i. Sequential dependency across rows: L row i needs rows j<i. If each rank has full matrix (replicated), and we assign row i to rank i%P, then rank owning row i needs rows 0..i-1 computed. Let's see:\n\nBlocked left-looking (like standard LAPACK potrf blocked):\nFor panel p (rows k..k+nb):\n  - Factor diagonal block A[k:k+nb, k:k+nb] (sequential within, bit-exact if sums ascending) — do redundantly on all ranks? Cost O(nb²*k) per panel, total O(nb*n²) — moderate; but redundant across P ranks wastes P times, yet it's a small fraction if nb small. Or compute on one rank and broadcast (sequential latency).\n  - Then for rows i >= k+nb (below panel), compute:\n      L[i, k:k+nb] = (A_orig[i, k:k+nb] - Σ_{t=0}^{k-1} L[i,t] * L[k:k+nb, t]^T) then triangular solve within panel.\n    The sum over t ascending from 0..k-1 then within panel columns. In original left-looking, row i's L[i,j] for j in panel is computed as sum over t=0..j-1 ascending, one continuous sum. Here we compute sum over t=0..k-1 (ascending) and then within-panel terms. The blocked approach: store partial sums? To match exactly, for each j in panel we need a single accumulation Σ_{t=0}^{j-1}. We can compute:\n       s_j = Σ_{t=0}^{k-1} L[i,t]*L[j,t]  (ascending)  ... but original continues adding t=k..j-1 in the same accumulator, not a separate sum. If we compute s_j = fl(Σ_{t=0}^{k-1}) and then continue adding t=k..j-1 to the same accumulator in ascending order, that is EXACTLY the same as original's single ascending sum! Because fl operations: acc = 0; add t=0..k-1; then add t=k..j-1. Yes identical if we carry the accumulator through the triangular solve. \n    So for each row i, for each jj in panel:\n       acc = 0; for t=0..k-1: acc += L[i,t]*L[j,t]  (L[j,t] panel row known after diag block)\n       for t=k..j-1: acc += L[i,t]*L[j,t]  (L[i,t] from this panel, computed earlier jj' < jj)\n       L[i,j] = (A_orig[i,j] - acc)/L[j,j]\n    This is bit-exact with original, provided:\n      - A_orig[i,j] available (local, yes if row i owned).\n      - L[i,t] for t<k available locally (row i local, and previously computed panel columns stored in local row).\n      - L[j,t] for t<k: these are rows j in current panel (k..k+nb-1) — previous columns belong to rows k..k+nb-1, which are computed by... these are the diagonal block rows and panel rows.   // panel rows: kb x rowLen\n    std::vector<double> BT(nb * n, 0.0);   // transposed: k (or rowLen) x kb; we need t in [0,k)\n    std::vector<double> Cbuf;              // not needed global\n    *failJ = -1;\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += nb) {\n        const size_t kb = std::min(nb, n-k);\n        const size_t rowLen = k + kb;\n        const int owner = int((k / nb) % P);\n        // locate local start row for k\n        // owner owns block k/nb, so rows k..k+kb-1 are local contiguous at index rk\n        bool localOk = true;\n        size_t rk = 0;\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            rk = std::lower_bound(l2g.begin(), l2g.end(), (int)k) - l2g.begin();\n            // diag block\n            for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb && localOk; ++jj) {\n                size_t j = k + jj;\n                double sum = 0.0;\n                for (size_t t = 0; t < j; ++t) {\n                    double v = A[(rk+jj)*n + t];\n                    sum += v*v;\n                }\n                double val = A[(rk+jj)*n + j] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) { localOk=false; *failJ=(int)j; break; }\n                A[(rk+jj)*n + j] = sqrt(val);\n                for (size_t ii = jj+1; ii < kb; ++ii) {\n                    double s2=0.0;\n                    const double* Ai=&A[(rk+ii)*n];\n                    const double* Aj=&A[(rk+jj)*n];\n                    for (size_t t=0;t<j;++t) s2 += Ai[t]*Aj[t];\n                    A[(rk+ii)*n+j] = (Ai[j]-s2)/A[(rk+jj)*n+j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        int flag = localOk?1:0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&flag,1,MPI_INT,owner,MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (!flag) { MPI_Bcast(failJ,1,MPI_INT,owner,MPI_COMM_WORLD); return false; }\n        // pack dd on owner\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            for (size_t ii=0; ii<kb; ++ii)\n                memcpy(&dd[ii*rowLen], &A[(rk+ii)*n], rowLen*sizeof(double));\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(dd.data(), (int)(kb*rowLen), MPI_DOUBLE, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        // Build BT: BT[t*kb + jj] = dd[jj*rowLen + t], for t in [0,k), jj in [0,kb)\n        for (size_t t=0;t<k;++t) {\n            double* bt = &BT[t*kb];\n            for (size_t jj=0;jj<kb;++jj) bt[jj] = dd[jj*rowLen + t];\n        }\n        // update local rows >= k+kb\n        const size_t thr = k + kb;\n        size_t start = std::lower_bound(l2g.begin(), l2g.end(), (int)thr) - l2g.begin();\n        const size_t MR = 16;\n        double Ctile[MR][?]; // kb variable up to nb; use constant NBMAX\n        ...\n        for (size_t r0 = start; r0 < lrows; r0 += MR) {\n            size_t mr = std::min(MR, lrows-r0);\n            // C tile mr x kb\n            double C[MR][64]; // NBMAX=64? but nb might be set to 64; ensure static max\n            memset...\n            for (size_t t=0;t<k;++t) {\n                const double* bt=&BT[t*kb];\n                for (size_t r=0;r<mr;++r) {\n                    double a = A[(r0+r)*n + t];\n                    double* c = C[r];\n                    for (size_t jj=0;jj<kb;++jj) c[jj] += a*bt[jj];\n                }\n            }\n            // triangular solve within panel\n            for (size_t r=0;r<mr;++r) {\n                double* Ai = &A[(r0+r)*n];\n                double* c = C[r];\n                for (size_t jj=0;jj<kb;++jj) {\n                    double acc = c[jj];\n                    for (size_t t=k;t<k+jj;++t) acc += Ai[t]*dd[jj*rowLen+t];\n                    Ai[k+jj] = (Ai[k+jj]-acc)/dd[jj*rowLen+(k+jj)];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n```\n\nNBMAX: choose nb always 64, define constexpr size_t BLOCK = 64. {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792585715646,"sessionID":"ses_ea19433bfffek1YVZ0XwF0u6Zn","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_00_xrq1l3eawosuh7oxx6tsxpdw","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/cholesky_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r3/cholesky/cholesky.cpp","content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Distributed-memory Cholesky decomposition.\n// The matrix is distributed over MPI ranks in block-cyclic row blocks; every\n// rank stores only the rows it owns. The blocked left-looking factorization\n// performs, for every element, exactly the same floating-point operations in\n// the same order as the original sequential algorithm, so the result does not\n// depend on the number of processes.\n\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr size_t kBlockSize = 64;\nconstexpr size_t kRowTile = 16;\n\ninline size_t blockBegin(size_t b, size_t nb) { return b * nb; }\n\ninline size_t blockEnd(size_t b, size_t n, size_t nb) {\n    return std::min(n, (b + 1) * nb);\n}\n\ninline size_t chooseBlockSize(size_t n) {\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return 1;\n    }\n    return std::min(n, kBlockSize);\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower\n// triangular. Only the locally owned rows of A are stored, in row-major order.\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, const size_t nb,\n                           const std::vector<int>& l2g, const int rank, const int numProcs,\n                           int& failIndex) {\n    const size_t lrows = l2g.size();\n    const size_t nblocks = (n + nb - 1) / nb;\n\n    std::vector<double> panel(nb * n, 0.0);\n    std::vector<double> panelT(nb * n, 0.0);\n\n    failIndex = -1;\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += nb) {\n        const size_t kb = std::min(nb, n - k);\n        const size_t rowLen = k + kb;\n        const size_t diagBlock = k / nb;\n        const int owner = static_cast<int>(diagBlock % static_cast<size_t>(numProcs));\n\n        size_t ownerRow = 0;\n        bool localOk = true;\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            ownerRow = static_cast<size_t>(\n                std::lower_bound(l2g.begin(), l2g.end(), static_cast<int>(k)) - l2g.begin());\n\n            for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb && localOk; ++jj) {\n                const size_t j = k + jj;\n                double sum = 0.0;\n                for (size_t t = 0; t < j; ++t) {\n                    const double v = A[(ownerRow + jj) * n + t];\n                    sum += v * v;\n                }\n                const double val = A[(ownerRow + jj) * n + j] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) {\n                    localOk = false;\n                    failIndex = static_cast<int>(j);\n                    break;\n                }\n                A[(ownerRow + jj) * n + j] = sqrt(val);\n\n                const double* Aj = &A[(ownerRow + jj) * n];\n                const double diag = A[(ownerRow + jj) * n + j];\n                for (size_t ii = jj + 1; ii < kb; ++ii) {\n                    const double* Ai = &A[(ownerRow + ii) * n];\n                    double s = 0.0;\n                    for (size_t t = 0; t < j; ++t) {\n                        s += Ai[t] * Aj[t];\n                    }\n                    A[(ownerRow + ii) * n + j] = (Ai[j] - s) / diag;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        int okFlag = localOk ? 1 : 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&okFlag, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (!okFlag) {\n            MPI_Bcast(&failIndex, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            for (size_t ii = 0; ii < kb; ++ii) {\n                std::memcpy(&panel[ii * rowLen], &A[(ownerRow + ii) * n],\n                            rowLen * sizeof(double));\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(panel.data(), static_cast<int>(kb * rowLen), MPI_DOUBLE, owner,\n                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < k; ++t) {\n            double* bt = &panelT[t * kb];\n            for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb; ++jj) {\n                bt[jj] = panel[jj * rowLen + t];\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t threshold = k + kb;\n        const size_t start = static_cast<size_t>(\n            std::lower_bound(l2g.begin(), l2g.end(), static_cast<int>(threshold)) - l2g.begin());\n\n        for (size_t r0 = start; r0 < lrows; r0 += kRowTile) {\n            const size_t mr = std::min(kRowTile, lrows - r0);\n            double c[kRowTile][kBlockSize];\n\n            for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                std::memset(c[r], 0, kb * sizeof(double));\n            }\n\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < k; ++t) {\n                const double* bt = &panelT[t * kb];\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                    const double a = A[(r0 + r) * n + t];\n                    double* cr = c[r];\n                    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb; ++jj) {\n                        cr[jj] += a * bt[jj];\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                double* Ai = &A[(r0 + r) * n];\n                double* cr = c[r];\n                for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb; ++jj) {\n                    double acc = cr[jj];\n                    for (size_t t = k; t < k + jj; ++t) {\n                        acc += Ai[t] * panel[jj * rowLen + t];\n                    }\n                    Ai[k + jj] = (Ai[k + jj] - acc) / panel[jj * rowLen + (k + jj)];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix distributed over row blocks.\n// The random sequence and the dot-product order match the sequential version.\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n,\n                                    const std::vector<int>& l2g) {\n    std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n    unsigned int seed = 42;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n    }\n\n    const size_t lrows = l2g.size();\n    for (size_t li = 0; li < lrows; ++li) {\n        const size_t gi = static_cast<size_t>(l2g[li]);\n        const double* Bi = &B[gi * n];\n        double* Ai = &A[li * n];\n\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double* Bj = &B[j * n];\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < n; ++t) {\n                sum += Bi[t] * Bj[t];\n            }\n            Ai[j] = sum;\n        }\n\n        Ai[gi] += static_cast<double>(n);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numProcs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numProcs);\n\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    bool badArgs = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = static_cast<size_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            badArgs = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (badArgs) {\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const size_t nb = chooseBlockSize(n);\n    const size_t nblocks = (n + nb - 1) / nb;\n\n    std::vector<int> l2g;\n    for (size_t b = static_cast<size_t>(rank); p < numProcs; ++p) {\n        rowDispls[p] = rowDispls[p - 1] + rowCounts[p - 1];\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> A(lrows * n, 0.0);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n    std::vector<double> gathered;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    }\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n, l2g);\n\n    auto gatherFull = [&](const std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& full) {\n        std::vector<int> counts(numProcs), displs(numProcs);\n        for (int p = 0; p < numProcs; ++p) {\n            counts[p] = rowCounts[p] * static_cast<int>(n);\n            displs[p] = rowDispls[p] * static_cast<int>(n);\n        }\n        std::vector<double> recv;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            recv.resize(n * n);\n        }\n        MPI_Gatherv(local.empty() ? nullptr : local.data(),\n                    static_cast<int>(local.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                    rank == 0 ? recv.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), displs.data(),\n                    MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            full.resize(n * n);\n            for (int p = 0; But if n%4!=0, need tail FMA per element—can handle t-outer for body (t < n&~3) and then a final pass over the last n%4 terms with explicit fma per j. That works and is exact for any n. And it's fast: for each row i, C[j] over all j, outer product over t: traffic B row i (n) + B all rows j (n²) → total n³/P MACs but SIMD across j, ~4 MACs per 2 instructions, maybe 2-4 GMAC/s. b += static_cast<size_t>(numProcs)) {\n        const size_t s = blockBegin(b, nb);\n        const size_t e = blockEnd(b, n, nb);\n        for (size_t r0 = s; r0 < e; r0 += kGenRows) {\n            const size_t mr = std::min(kGenRows, e - r0);\n            for (size_t j0 = s; j0 < n; j0 += kGenCols) {\n                const size_t nc = std::min(kGenCols, n - j0);\n                if (j0 + nc <= r0) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                double c[kGenRows][kGenCols];\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                    std::memset(c[r], 0, nc * sizeof(double));\n                }\n                for (size_t t = 0; t < body; ++t) {\n                    const double* row = &BT[t * n];\n                    for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                        const double a = row[r0 + r];\n                        double* cr = c[r];\n                        for (size_t jj = 0; jj < nc; ++jj) {\n                            cr[jj] += a * row[j0 + jj];\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                for (size_t t = body; t < n; ++t) {\n                    const double* row = &BT[t * n];\n                    for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                        const double a = row[r0 + r];\n                        double* cr = c[r];\n                        for (size_t jj = 0; jj < nc; ++jj) {\n                            cr[jj] = std::fma(a, row[j0 + jj], cr[jj]);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                    const size_t gi = r0 + r;\n                    double* Ai = &A[(localBase + (gi - s)) * n];\n                    const size_t jstart = std::max(j0, gi);\n                    for (size_t j = jstart; j < j0 + nc; ++j) {\n                        Ai[j] = c[r][j - j0];\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        localBase += e - s;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double>().swap(BT);\n\n    std::vector<int> counts(numProcs), displs(numProcs);\n    for (int p = 0; p < numProcs; ++p) {\n        counts[p] = rowCounts[p] * static_cast<int>(n);\n        displs[p] = rowDispls[p] * static_cast<int>(n);\n    }\n    std::vector<double> upper(n * n);\n    MPI_Allgatherv(A.empty() ? nullptr : A.data(), static_cast<int>(A.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                   upper.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    for (size_t li = 0; li < lrows; ++li) {\n        const size_t gi = static_cast<size_t>(l2g[li]);\n        double* Ai = &A[li * n];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < gi; ++j) {\n            Ai[j] = upper[j * n + gi];\n        }\n        Ai[gi] += static_cast<double>(n);\n    }\n}\n\n'''\ns=s[:start]+new+s[end:]\n# update call in main\ns=s.replace('generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n, l2g);','generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n, nb, l2g, rank, numProcs, rowCounts, rowDispls);')\nopen('/tmp/extgen.cpp','w').write(s)\nEOF\nmpicxx -O3 -march=native -ffp-contract=off -std=c++20 -o /tmp/extgen /tmp/extgen.cpp -lm 2>&1 | head -20","time":{"start":1792589442698,"end":1792589446459}},"id":"prt_15ea8200f001AQqERDwvqnL558","sessionID":"ses_ea19433bfffek1YVZ0XwF0u6Zn","messageID":"msg_15ea7e035001fpHHxd9VVmVlA4"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792590252604,"sessionID":"ses_ea19433bfffek1YVZ0XwF0u6Zn","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"call_00_j0cwveypaum2p84n70w9mwsp","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"content":"#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Distributed-memory Cholesky decomposition.\n//\n// The matrix is partitioned over the MPI ranks with a block-cyclic row\n// distribution, so every rank stores only the rows it owns.  The factorization\n// is a blocked left-looking algorithm: for each panel only the rows of that\n// panel are broadcast, and all ranks update their own rows concurrently.  The\n// accumulation order is identical to the sequential algorithm, therefore the\n// result is bit-for-bit independent of the number of processes.\n\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr size_t kBlockSize = 16;\nconstexpr size_t kRowTile = 16;\n\ninline size_t blockBegin(size_t b, size_t nb) { return b * nb; }\n\ninline size_t blockEnd(size_t b, size_t n, size_t nb) {\n    return std::min(n, (b + 1) * nb);\n}\n\ninline size_t chooseBlockSize(size_t n) {\n    if (n == 0) {\n        return 1;\n    }\n    return std::min(n, kBlockSize);\n}\n\n// Reproduces the floating-point reduction performed by the original scalar\n// loop: the bulk is accumulated with separately rounded products, followed by\n// a fused multiply-add tail.\ninline double dotSequential(const double* a, const double* b, size_t len) {\n    double s = 0.0;\n    const size_t body = (len >= 4) ? (len & ~static_cast<size_t>(3)) : 0;\n    for (size_t t = 0; t < body; ++t) {\n        s += a[t] * b[t];\n    }\n    for (size_t t = body; t < len; ++t) {\n        s = std::fma(a[t], b[t], s);\n    }\n    return s;\n}\n\n} // namespace\n\n// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower\n// triangular. Only the locally owned rows of A are stored, in row-major order.\nbool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, const size_t nb,\n                           const std::vector<int>& l2g, const int rank, const int numProcs,\n                           int& failIndex) {\n    const size_t lrows = l2g.size();\n\n    std::vector<double> panel(nb * n, 0.0);\n    std::vector<double> panelT(nb * n, 0.0);\n\n    failIndex = -1;\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < n; k += nb) {\n        const size_t kb = std::min(nb, n - k);\n        const size_t rowLen = k + kb;\n        const size_t diagBlock = k / nb;\n        const int owner = static_cast<int>(diagBlock % static_cast<size_t>(numProcs));\n\n        size_t ownerRow = 0;\n        bool localOk = true;\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            ownerRow = static_cast<size_t>(\n                std::lower_bound(l2g.begin(), l2g.end(), static_cast<int>(k)) - l2g.begin());\n\n            for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb && localOk; ++jj) {\n                const size_t j = k + jj;\n                const double* Aj = &A[(ownerRow + jj) * n];\n                const double sum = dotSequential(Aj, Aj, j);\n                const double val = Aj[j] - sum;\n                if (val <= 0.0) {\n                    localOk = false;\n                    failIndex = static_cast<int>(j);\n                    break;\n                }\n                A[(ownerRow + jj) * n + j] = sqrt(val);\n\n                const double diag = A[(ownerRow + jj) * n + j];\n                for (size_t ii = jj + 1; ii < kb; ++ii) {\n                    const double* Ai = &A[(ownerRow + ii) * n];\n                    const double s = dotSequential(Ai, Aj, j);\n                    A[(ownerRow + ii) * n + j] = (Ai[j] - s) / diag;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        int okFlag = localOk ? 1 : 0;\n        MPI_Bcast(&okFlag, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (!okFlag) {\n            MPI_Bcast(&failIndex, 1, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n            return false;\n        }\n\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            for (size_t ii = 0; ii < kb; ++ii) {\n                std::memcpy(&panel[ii * rowLen], &A[(ownerRow + ii) * n],\n                            rowLen * sizeof(double));\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(panel.data(), static_cast<int>(kb * rowLen), MPI_DOUBLE, owner,\n                  MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < k; ++t) {\n            double* bt = &panelT[t * kb];\n            for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb; ++jj) {\n                bt[jj] = panel[jj * rowLen + t];\n            }\n        }\n\n        const size_t threshold = k + kb;\n        const size_t start = static_cast<size_t>(\n            std::lower_bound(l2g.begin(), l2g.end(), static_cast<int>(threshold)) - l2g.begin());\n\n        for (size_t r0 = start; r0 < lrows; r0 += kRowTile) {\n            const size_t mr = std::min(kRowTile, lrows - r0);\n            double c[kRowTile][kBlockSize];\n\n            for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                std::memset(c[r], 0, kb * sizeof(double));\n            }\n\n            for (size_t t = 0; t < k; ++t) {\n                const double* bt = &panelT[t * kb];\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                    const double a = A[(r0 + r) * n + t];\n                    double* cr = c[r];\n                    for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb; ++jj) {\n                        cr[jj] += a * bt[jj];\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                double* Ai = &A[(r0 + r) * n];\n                double* cr = c[r];\n                for (size_t jj = 0; jj < kb; ++jj) {\n                    double acc = cr[jj];\n                    const size_t len = k + jj;\n                    const size_t qin = (len >= 4) ? (jj & ~static_cast<size_t>(3)) : 0;\n                    const double* Pj = &panel[jj * rowLen];\n                    for (size_t t = k; t < k + qin; ++t) {\n                        acc += Ai[t] * Pj[t];\n                    }\n                    for (size_t t = k + qin; t < len; ++t) {\n                        acc = std::fma(Ai[t], Pj[t], acc);\n                    }\n                    Ai[len] = (Ai[len] - acc) / Pj[len];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix distributed over row blocks.\n// Each rank computes the upper-triangular part of the rows it owns (symmetry\n// halves the work); the lower part is then exchanged. The accumulation order\n// matches the sequential version exactly.\nvoid generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, const size_t nb,\n                                    const std::vector<int>& l2g, const int rank,\n                                    const int numProcs, const std::vector<int>& rowCounts,\n                                    const std::vector<int>& rowDispls) {\n    const size_t lrows = l2g.size();\n    const size_t nblocks = (n + nb - 1) / nb;\n\n    std::vector<double> BT(n * n, 0.0);\n    {\n        std::vector<double> B(n * n);\n        unsigned int seed = 42;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n            B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;\n        }\n        const size_t tile = 32;\n        for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < n; i0 += tile) {\n            const size_t ie = std::min(n, i0 + tile);\n            for (size_t j0 = 0; j0 < n; j0 += tile) {\n                const size_t je = std::min(n, j0 + tile);\n                for (size_t i = i0; i < ie; ++i) {\n                    for (size_t j = j0; j < je; ++j) {\n                        BT[j * n + i] = B[i * n + j];\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t body = (n >= 4) ? (n & ~static_cast<size_t>(3)) : 0;\n    constexpr size_t kGenRows = 16;\n    constexpr size_t kGenCols = 128;\n\n    size_t localBase = 0;\n    for (size_t b = static_cast<size_t>(rank); b < nblocks; b += static_cast<size_t>(numProcs)) {\n        const size_t s = blockBegin(b, nb);\n        const size_t e = blockEnd(b, n, nb);\n        for (size_t r0 = s; r0 < e; r0 += kGenRows) {\n            const size_t mr = std::min(kGenRows, e - r0);\n            for (size_t j0 = s; j0 < n; j0 += kGenCols) {\n                const size_t nc = std::min(kGenCols, n - j0);\n                if (j0 + nc <= r0) {\n                    continue;\n                }\n                double c[kGenRows][kGenCols];\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                    std::memset(c[r], 0, nc * sizeof(double));\n                }\n                for (size_t t = 0; t < body; ++t) {\n                    const double* row = &BT[t * n];\n                    for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                        const double a = row[r0 + r];\n                        double* cr = c[r];\n                        for (size_t jj = 0; jj < nc; ++jj) {\n                            cr[jj] += a * row[j0 + jj];\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                for (size_t t = body; t < n; ++t) {\n                    const double* row = &BT[t * n];\n                    for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                        const double a = row[r0 + r];\n                        double* cr = c[r];\n                        for (size_t jj = 0; jj < nc; ++jj) {\n                            cr[jj] = std::fma(a, row[j0 + jj], cr[jj]);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < mr; ++r) {\n                    const size_t gi = r0 + r;\n                    double* Ai = &A[(localBase + (gi - s)) * n];\n                    const size_t jstart = std::max(j0, gi);\n                    for (size_t j = jstart; j < j0 + nc; ++j) {\n                        Ai[j] = c[r][j - j0];\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        localBase += e - s;\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double>().swap(BT);\n\n    std::vector<int> counts(numProcs), displs(numProcs);\n    for (int p = 0; p < numProcs; ++p) {\n        counts[p] = rowCounts[p] * static_cast<int>(n);\n        displs[p] = rowDispls[p] * static_cast<int>(n);\n    }\n    std::vector<double> recv(n * n);\n    MPI_Allgatherv(A.empty() ? nullptr : A.data(), static_cast<int>(A.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                   recv.data(), counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    std::vector<double> upper(n * n);\n    for (int p = 0; p < numProcs; ++p) {\n        size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(rowDispls[p]);\n        for (size_t b = static_cast<size_t>(p); b < nblocks; b += static_cast<size_t>(numProcs)) {\n            for (size_t g = blockBegin(b, nb); g < blockEnd(b, n, nb); ++g, ++c) {\n                std::memcpy(&upper[g * n], &recv[c * n], n * sizeof(double));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (size_t li = 0; li < lrows; ++li) {\n        const size_t gi = static_cast<size_t>(l2g[li]);\n        double* Ai = &A[li * n];\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < gi; ++j) {\n            Ai[j] = upper[j * n + gi];\n        }\n        Ai[gi] += static_cast<double>(n);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {\n    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix\n\n    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);\n\n    // Compute L * L^T\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {\n                sum += L[i * n + k] * L[j * n + k];\n            }\n            reconstructed[i * n + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Compare with original\n    double maxError = 0.0;\n    double relError = 0.0;\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {\n        const double error = fabs(reconstructed[i] - A_orig[i]);\n        maxError = std::max(maxError, error);\n\n        const double rel = error / (fabs(A_orig[i]) + 1e-10);\n        relError = std::max(relError, rel);\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Max absolute error: %.10e\\n\", maxError);\n    printf(\"Max relative error: %.10e\\n\", relError);\n\n    // Check if error is within tolerance\n    if (relError > 1e-6) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: relative error too large\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numProcs = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numProcs);\n\n    size_t n = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    bool badArgs = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n = static_cast<size_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            badArgs = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (badArgs) {\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 1;\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Cholesky Decomposition Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", n, n);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const size_t nb = chooseBlockSize(n);\n    const size_t nblocks = (n + nb - 1) / nb;\n\n    std::vector<int> l2g;\n    for (size_t b = static_cast<size_t>(rank); b < nblocks; b += static_cast<size_t>(numProcs)) {\n        for (size_t g = blockBegin(b, nb); g < blockEnd(b, n, nb); ++g) {\n            l2g.push_back(static_cast<int>(g));\n        }\n    }\n    const size_t lrows = l2g.size();\n\n    std::vector<int> rowCounts(numProcs, 0);\n    std::vector<int> rowDispls(numProcs, 0);\n    const int localRows = static_cast<int>(lrows);\n    MPI_Allgather(&localRows, 1, MPI_INT, rowCounts.data(), 1, MPI_INT, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    for (int p = 1; p < numProcs; ++p) {\n        rowDispls[p] = rowDispls[p - 1] + rowCounts[p - 1];\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> A(lrows * n, 0.0);\n    std::vector<double> A_orig;\n    std::vector<double> gathered;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Generating positive definite matrix...\\n\");\n    }\n    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n, nb, l2g, rank, numProcs, rowCounts, rowDispls);\n\n    auto gatherFull = [&](const std::vector<double>& local, std::vector<double>& full) {\n        std::vector<int> counts(numProcs), displs(numProcs);\n        for (int p = 0; p < numProcs; ++p) {\n            counts[p] = rowCounts[p] * static_cast<int>(n);\n            displs[p] = rowDispls[p] * static_cast<int>(n);\n        }\n        std::vector<double> recv;\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            recv.resize(n * n);\n        }\n        MPI_Gatherv(local.empty() ? nullptr : local.data(),\n                    static_cast<int>(local.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                    rank == 0 ? recv.data() : nullptr, counts.data(), displs.data(),\n                    MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            full.resize(n * n);\n            for (int p = 0;
